# Contradictions and collisions

What the inventoried artifacts give differently, found and checked on 2026-10-23 for the Artifact Textbook (issue #120, finished under #136). Readers went over the inventory's numbers table (3,025 numbers from 69 artifacts) for every quantity two artifacts give with different values, and over each topic cluster's full records for disagreements, revised figures, stale statements and shared unsourced claims. A script then looked for every candidate's quotes in the artifact's extracted text and, failing that, in the page's own files; the columns 'Quote A' and 'Quote B' say what it found. Last, a skeptic read both source texts for each cluster candidate and for each number collision a reader classed as a contradiction or a later revision, and gave a verdict: confirmed (both say what the candidate says and they disagree), superseded (the later one says or plainly implies it revises the earlier), refuted (they agree once read in context, or a statement is not there) or cannot tell. A blank verdict means no skeptic read that candidate; the reader's kind is then all there is. Nothing here is copied from memory, people are named by role, and no address of any kind is reproduced.

## Confirmed: what a planner must not take from one artifact alone

Every disagreement a skeptic confirmed or found revised, with the value or statement it says the book should carry. The full candidate, with both quotes, is in the sections below.

### Level naming and counting

1. Huang 2022 registration-free cadaver long-film accuracy (88.3%) as the published comparator (confirmed; registration-free-level-id-feasibility-uramo1 and naming-vertebrae-without-ct-hjy4vc). Carry 88.3% only as the figure Huang 2022 reports on 100 cadaveric Long-Film images, marked unverified as the feasibility page says, until the paper's full text is read.
2. C-ArmLSpine case count (confirmed; counting-from-the-sacrum and or-readiness-ledger). The fold file of the five-fold cross-validation would settle it. Until then carry 148 cases from the ledger (split 105/15/28), give 149 as the dataset's case count, and flag 141.
3. Whole-lateral-frame exact-level accuracy on C-ArmLSpine (count alone, coupling, bodies vs frames) (superseded-by-later; counting-from-the-sacrum and handoff-schwingnet-ctspinopelvic1k-and-the-imaging-programme-rbcdmf). Carry 97.4% from the handoff (2026-10-08) for fine-tuned naming on whole lateral frames. Give 98.2% only as the coupled count on anchored bodies, with its 3-case gain over the count alone.
4. Zero-shot AP naming accuracy on C-ArmLSpine (superseded-by-later; counting-from-the-sacrum and any-view-spine-plan). Carry 43.7% from the any-view plan (2026-10-21).
5. One-vertebra-in-field naming accuracy (superseded-by-later; counting-from-the-sacrum and handoff-schwingnet-ctspinopelvic1k-and-the-imaging-programme-rbcdmf). Carry 74.8% from the handoff (2026-10-08).
6. Zero-shot lateral naming on C-ArmLSpine (superseded-by-later; counting-from-the-sacrum and any-view-spine-plan). Carry 96.4% (595/617) from the any-view plan.
7. Held-out-device vertebra detection recall on C-ArmLSpine (superseded-by-later; or-readiness-ledger and any-view-spine-plan). Carry 80.5% from the handoff (2026-10-08) as held-out-device recall, noting that the ledger's 90.6% was an outline-convention re-score on 56 frames. The catalog row's truth convention would explain the gap.
8. Population-matched render-trained sacrum detection on DeepFluoro real frames (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Carry 90-97% from counting-levels, with its table, unless the run file shows 96-98%. The proposal's range needs a source or a correction.
9. Fraction of a real frame's variance the physics renderer explains (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Carry counting-levels' corrected values: 86% for physics alone and about 90% for the calibrated renderer. Neither page says whether the 93.9% smooth-field figure also moves.
10. What decides the level: counting from an anchor versus naming from morphology (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and naming-vertebrae-without-ct-hjy4vc). Present them as successive designs: naming from morphology (2026-09-18), then counting from the anchor (counting-levels, AIDaS). Give the handoff's combined model as the current state.
11. Sweep chain-intact rate by overlap (half, one, two, four bodies) (superseded-by-later; naming-vertebrae-without-an-anchor-3dfzmh and vertebral-levels-without-ct-a4rydj). Carry the re-measured table of 2026-09-18 for accuracy and exposures. Flag the chain-intact column, where the naming page's values differ.
12. Thoracic two-vertebra naming with and without the sweep (superseded-by-later; naming-vertebrae-without-an-anchor-3dfzmh and vertebral-levels-without-ct-a4rydj). Carry the re-measured n=12 result and the naming page's wider table. The two September-18 pages disagree on the same 12 studies (4.3->91.3 vs 8.3->91.7), so the evaluation file should decide.
13. Lumbar two-vertebra naming on CT renders with the sweep (superseded-by-later; naming-vertebrae-without-an-anchor-3dfzmh and naming-vertebrae-without-ct-hjy4vc). Carry 76.9% to 93.9% on 118 held-out lumbar CT renders (2026-09-18).

Carry the handoff's detector description. Give the field report's scores only as an early in-distribution result.

### Transitional vertebrae and border variants

48. Number of lumbar-rib cases in CTSpinoPelvic1K (802 records) (confirmed; counting-against-two-anchors-qkktgu and spinal-border-variants-fye5ef). Carry one count of lumbar-rib cases from the current label release (v11 per the 2026-10-08 pages), with the rule stated (rib articulating with a lumbar body, by case). A recount on v11 settles it; until then the book should give 13 (v5, renumbered) and 16 (release unnamed) side by side with their sources.
49. Lumbar-rib count in the project's own CTs as tabled for training (confirmed; overnight-the-honest-99-kantj8 and spinal-border-variants-fye5ef). Recount lumbar-rib cases on the release the training actually uses (v11), and give the review sheet's count beside it. The book should not carry 16 as a training denominator without the overnight page's own warning attached.
50. Co-occurrence of thoracolumbar and lumbosacral variants within CTSpinoPelvic1K (the both-cell and its odds ratio) (confirmed; spinal-border-variants-fye5ef and naming-vertebrae-without-an-anchor-3dfzmh). The book should carry the naming page's label-voxel figure with its definition, and present the border page's 19.6 only with its definition stated and its own caveat ('an association in segmentation-derived measures'). Running both flag definitions on the same v11 labels and tabling the 2x2 for each would settle it.
51. Strength of thoracolumbar-lumbosacral co-occurrence used as the programme's number (confirmed; spinal-border-variants-fye5ef and naming-vertebrae-without-an-anchor-3dfzmh). Carry the naming page's graded estimates (3.61 VerSe, 2.1 with both junctions in field, 4.00 CTSpinoPelvic1K) as the programme's number. Report 19.6 only as a segmentation-derived association under its own definition. CT-3's whole-cohort read is the planned test that settles it on CTSpinoPelvic1K.
52. Count of thoracolumbar-variant spines in CTSpinoPelvic1K (confirmed; spinal-border-variants-fye5ef and naming-vertebrae-without-an-anchor-3dfzmh). State one definition of a thoracolumbar variant (label-voxel T13, lumbar rib or rib count, as on the naming page, or a rib-12 length threshold) and recount CTSpinoPelvic1K under it. The book should not merge the two counts.
53. Distribution of the non-rib-bearing vertebra count (cases at four) in CTSpinoPelvic1K (confirmed; rib-anchor-audit-t7wr2h and spinal-border-variants-fye5ef). Recompute the count histogram on the current release with the audit's nearest-vertebra rib-bearing rule, crossed with the LSTV label. Carry the audit's caveat that the distribution largely reflects the label convention.
54. Whether the non-rib-bearing vertebra count carries the lumbosacral phenotype (confirmed; rib-anchor-audit-t7wr2h and spinal-border-variants-fye5ef). Carry the audit's verdict and its radiologist comparison. Present the border page's d 0.8 only as separation of a label from a measure derived from the same labels. Rescoring the count against the readers' Lumbosac.csv column on the current release would settle whether the verdict still stands.
55. Whether the CTSpinoPelvic1K label volume ever carries a sixth lumbar vertebra (confirmed; rib-anchor-audit-t7wr2h and spinal-border-variants-fye5ef). Correct the audit's sentence to say that most, not all, transitional cases are labelled L1-L5 plus sacrum, and carry the L6 label count (18 by release label on v11) beside it.
56. Four pairs of sacral foramina is normal and five means the sacrum absorbed a lumbar segment (confirmed; counting-against-two-anchors-qkktgu and spinal-border-variants-fye5ef). Cite an anatomy reference for the four-pair rule before the book states it. Report the corpus mode of three as an effect of truncation and the detector, not as anatomy.
57. The ilium's auricular surface and the sacroiliac articulation are unchanged by lumbosacral fusion and can anchor sacralisation (confirmed; counting-against-two-anchors-qkktgu and rib-anchor-audit-t7wr2h). Cite the anatomical and Castellvi-definition sources, and run the audit's two proposed mask tests (ilium contact, SI height against S1) before the book uses the SI anchor as established.
58. Ribs on the wrong vertebra and misnumbered cases in CTSpinoPelvic1K v5 (superseded-by-later; rib-anchor-audit-t7wr2h and spinal-border-variants-fye5ef). Carry 19 ribs on the wrong vertebra and 2 misnumbered cases after the renumbering, with the audit's 170 to 109 as the earlier step. Check that the renumbering reached the released labels (v11) before treating 19 as the training labels' error count.
59. Prevalence of a sixth lumbar vertebra (confirmed; counting-against-two-anchors-qkktgu and state-of-the-literature-datasets-held-missing-size-and-power-8hg1ay). Carry 3.2% (Sun 2025) as the population prior and 2.0% as CTSpinoPelvic1K's read, each with its source. Drop the unsourced 'roughly 2% of people' or relabel it as the corpus rate.
60. Number of L6 cases in CTSpinoPelvic1K (confirmed; spine-program-decisions-qqhkqz and state-of-the-literature-datasets-held-missing-size-and-power-8hg1ay). 61. Number of four-lumbar (sacralised L5) cases in CTSpinoPelvic1K (confirmed; overnight-the-honest-99-kantj8 and research-directions-from-the-data-holding-kcyckb). The T12-versus-L1 AUC 0.990 figure: its vertebra count and whether it is a single-vertebra result (confirmed; counting-against-two-anchors-qkktgu and research-directions-from-the-data-holding-kcyckb). 68. Castellvi type I is about two thirds (68%) of all lumbosacral transitional anatomy (confirmed; overnight-the-honest-99-kantj8 and research-directions-from-the-data-holding-kcyckb). 69. The VerSe rare-class precedent: how many L6 training cases make L6 learnable (confirmed; overnight-the-honest-99-kantj8 and state-of-the-literature-datasets-held-missing-size-and-power-8hg1ay). Carry the claim once, cited to Kwak 2025 as the feasibility page gives it, and drop the 'population prevalence' reading of BUU's 4.0% (see the prevalence candidate).

The programme's headline 2-D naming figures and the encoder leak (confirmed; handoff-schwingnet-ctspinopelvic1k-and-the-imaging-programme-rbcdmf and research-directions-from-the-data-holding-kcyckb). Cite the figures once, to the underlying HANDOFF files in spinesurg-ct-nnunet that both pages name, and do not count the two pages as independent confirmation.
72. The COBRA one-labeller screen precision and the transitional power targets (confirmed; state-of-the-literature-datasets-held-missing-size-and-power-8hg1ay and handoff-schwingnet-ctspinopelvic1k-and-the-imaging-programme-rbcdmf). Cite once, to HANDOFF/LITERATURE_STATE.md in spinesurg-ct-nnunet as the state-of-the-literature page names it.

### Datasets, holdings and licences

### FluoroCap: the corpus, its pipeline and its audits

110. Figure-mate region agreement with constraint decoding versus the level model alone (same corpus, same date, 2026-09-14) (confirmed; fluorocap-dataset-card-xwd4wx and fluorocap-corpus-audit-9hjzzs). Carry: 93.1% on 2,143 held-out film-manifest items, not FluoroCap panels. The classifier is unvalidated on the corpus until a labelled FluoroCap sample is scored, and the book should name which classifier version each number belongs to.
113. Caption-held-out accuracy of the level model alone, and containment of its level set (superseded-by-later; fluorocap-corpus-audit-9hjzzs and fluorocap-reality-check-czudvb). The random draw on the level verification bench, once it has been read, gives the first accuracy estimate.

Withdraw the card's open-licence sentence. 115. Rule for sources whose terms forbid automated collection (confirmed; fluorocap-access-setup-jlvkft and schwingnet-data-registry). ### Renders, physics and the sim-to-real gap

243. Fraction of a real DeepFluoro frame's variance explained by physics alone (and by the calibrated renderer held out) (superseded-by-later; ninety-three-pixels and counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy). Carry 86% for physics alone and about 90% for the calibrated renderer on a held-out frame, both from Counting Levels Under Fluoroscopy. Keep 80.9% only as the figure from before the log-compression correction.
244. Physics-only variance explained and the smooth-field figure quoted in the October proposal and the IRB draft (confirmed; aidas-seed-grant-proposal and counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy). Carry 86% physics-only (calibrated renderer about 90% held out) from Counting Levels Under Fluoroscopy. Flag the proposal and the IRB draft's 80.9% as uncorrected.
245. What remains at bone scale after physics and the per-frame field: 'nothing local' versus 2 to 7 times the noise floor (superseded-by-later; ninety-three-pixels and what-is-left-after-physics). Carry What Is Left's reading: after the smooth field, 0.1-0.8% of variance per band at 2-16 px, two to seven times the noise floor, at cortical edges and bowel gas. Drop 'nothing local'.
246. Sacrum recall on real DeepFluoro frames of a detector trained only on renders of DeepFluoro's own CTs (population-matched, leave-one-specimen-out) (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Carry 90-97% from Counting Levels, which names its runs (21 Sep, tools/deepfluoro_anchor_report.py), unless a later run record shows 96-98%. Check that report's output.
247. Whether per-vertebra detection recall on real C-arm fluoroscopy has been published (confirmed; ninety-three-pixels and counting-from-the-sacrum). Carry neither without qualification. Find the source behind 0.96-0.98 (docs/RSNAI_CLAIMS.json), and whether it is recall on real C-arm frames, before the textbook states either way.
248. Best published CT-free (registration-free) level naming on a real intraoperative modality (confirmed; counting-from-the-sacrum and fluorocap-reality-check-czudvb). Carry 88.3% (Huang 2022, uninstrumented cadaver Long-Film) from the Reality Check, with its scope. Source the 73.3% or narrow its scope (C-arm frames, patients) before keeping it.
249. SyntheX: size of the scaled render set and whether randomisation or scale carried the win (confirmed; lift-before-label-jzjq8t and fluorocap-reality-check-czudvb). Carry the Reality Check's verified reading: parity at 6.44 vs 6.46 mm under strong randomisation, and 5.95 mm with the scaled set (reported as 10,000 or 18,000), the win coming from scale.
250. Whether a learned DRR-to-real refiner was built and measured (superseded-by-later; ninety-three-pixels and counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy). Carry Counting Levels' result: refiners and the low-band swap add 8-10 points, each smaller than the seed spread, and the refined render agrees slightly less with the real frame (68.1% vs 69.3%).
251. Status of the learned refiner and Fourier low-band swap arms (confirmed; aidas-seed-grant-proposal and counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy). Carry the measured arms from Counting Levels Under Fluoroscopy and mark the proposal's 'results are pending' as stale.
252. FluoroGen (SDXL image-to-image restyling of DRRs) as a live training loop (superseded-by-later; fluorocap-pipeline-map-h9rmbs and fluorocap-overnight-report-xnhgdo). Carry the Overnight Report (15 Sep): FluoroGen img2img dropped, 12,896 images kept for inspection only. The map's FluoroGen lane is historical.
253. FluoroCap panel count on disk (superseded-by-later; fluorocap-overnight-report-xnhgdo and fluorocap-reality-check-czudvb). Carry 19,581 as the 15 Sep count (Reality Check), and 29,500 (registry, 20 Oct) as the latest panel count.
254. DeepDRR's naive-to-physics landmark error improvement (78.2 to 24.7 mm) (confirmed; what-is-left-after-physics and lift-before-label-jzjq8t). Carry it once, as Unberath 2019: 78.2 to 34.4 to 24.7 mm on 78 real cadaver frames, scatter 26.9 to 24.7 (What Is Left's breakdown).
255. SyntheX's CycleGAN and ADDA arms against no adaptation (confirmed; ninety-three-pixels and what-is-left-after-physics). Carry it once, cited to SyntheX (Gao 2023), with What Is Left's weak-randomisation note.
256. Preliminary-results paragraph (99.17-100% walks, 99.14 vs 71.67% recall, 80.9%, 93.9/99.5%, 96-98%, 64-77%, 26 points, 5-60%) (confirmed; aidas-seed-grant-proposal and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). 257. Wrong-level surgery registry and fiducial-cost paragraph (34,544 operations, 0.12%, 81.3%, 51.9 min, 183 mGy, $1,750-1,950) (confirmed; aidas-seed-grant-proposal and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). 261. Registration-derived labels are biased toward registration-friendly frames (confirmed; lift-before-label-jzjq8t and lift-before-label-jzjq8t). Carry it as an unsourced caution from Lift Before Label. A measurement of label accuracy on frames that register poorly, or a citation, would turn it into a finding.
262. The three direct tests 'next' after the residual decomposition (confirmed; what-is-left-after-physics and counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy). Carry Counting Levels' low-band-swap and population-matched results. Keep the other two tests (miss-to-field correlation, dividing out the field at inference) as unmeasured.

### The case for the OR study: proposal, seed grant and IRB protocols

265. DeepFluoro sacrum recall for a detector trained on renders of DeepFluoro's own CTs (leave-one-specimen-out) (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Carry 90-97% from Counting Levels Under Fluoroscopy, the figure tied to the dated runs and a named report tool. Use 96-98% only if a run record for it turns up.
266. Fraction of a real frame's variance explained by the physics renderer (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Carry the corrected reading from Counting Levels Under Fluoroscopy (86% physics alone, about 90% calibrated on held-out frames). Mark the grant's and protocol's 80.9% as the figure from before the correction.
267. Results of the learned refiners and the Fourier low-band swap (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Carry the measured results from Counting Levels Under Fluoroscopy, with its caveat that every gain is inside the seed spread. Drop the grant's 'results are pending'.
268. Where the registry's wrong-level events occurred (confirmed; protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p and level-counting-visual-abstract). Carry the regional rates from the grant and the visual abstract. Drop 'every event was at L4-5 or L5-S1' unless the registry paper supports it for some subset.
269. What $1,750-1,950 per fiducial placement means (confirmed; level-counting-visual-abstract and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Carry the grant's derivation: about $1,750-1,950 per placement, which is 51.9 minutes of OR time at the FY2014 cost, not a price for the device.
270. IRB review route for the retrospective fluoroscopy collection (superseded-by-later; protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Carry Expedited Category 5 from the Retrospective Fluoroscopy Protocol. Note that its FDA-ineligibility reason cites no regulation (see candidate 48).
271. Records requested and the date window of the retrospective pull (superseded-by-later; protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Carry the retrospective protocol's scope (one surgeon's spine cases, 2021-2026, N to confirm by pilot query). Treat the 2006 window and the 40,000-study figure as the superseded design.
272. Study duration (superseded-by-later; protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Carry the retrospective protocol's plan (steps 2-11 within six months, proposed end two years after approval, to confirm). Leave out the draft's three years.
273. Reference standard for level truth (superseded-by-later; protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Carry the retrospective protocol's reference standard (operative note, post-operative imaging where needed) and its H1 of at least 97% recall. Mark the CT-registration standard as the earlier design.
274. Sample size for the primary endpoint (superseded-by-later; protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Carry about 1,100 labelled held-out bodies (97%, half-width 1 point) from the Retrospective Fluoroscopy Protocol. Note that its case count waits on the 20-case pilot query.
275. Frames and patients the pull should yield (confirmed; protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p and fluorocap-reality-check-czudvb). Carry neither as settled. The pilot PACS query of 20 cases named in the Retrospective Fluoroscopy Protocol (frames per case, share missing from PACS) decides the yield.
276. Whether Protocol 2 (shadow-mode) is submitted with Protocol 1 (superseded-by-later; protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Carry the retrospective protocol's sequence: Protocol 2 is submitted separately, after Protocol 1 meets its endpoints.
277. Number of C-ArmLSpine cases in the five-fold evaluation (confirmed; counting-from-the-sacrum and or-readiness-ledger). Settle it with the fold lists of the cross-validation (cases with a lateral frame scored). Until then, carry 98.2% with the case count marked unresolved (141 or 148 cases; 120 distinct subjects).
278. What SchwingNet does: count without naming, or name without counting (superseded-by-later; aidas-seed-grant-proposal and vertebral-levels-without-ct-a4rydj). Carry 'count, not name' from the grant and the protocol as the method under validation. Treat Vertebral Levels Without CT as an earlier, naming-based design.
279. Huang 2022 registration-free naming at 88.3% (confirmed; fluorocap-reality-check-czudvb and registration-free-level-id-feasibility-uramo1). Do not carry 88.3% as a verified comparator. Describe the 2022 study by its protocol alone, as the Registration-Free Level ID Feasibility note does.
280. Fifteen business days as the lead time for dosimetry, training and radiation-safety steps (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Cite the OEHS Radiation Safety policy document and its section on dosimetry and training lead times before the textbook carries the fifteen days.
281. Joint Commission 2024 sentinel-event counts and NHS England never events (confirmed; aidas-seed-grant-proposal and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Cite the Joint Commission's 2024 sentinel event report and NHS England's 2024/25 never-events data, and check the 36% harm figure in them.
282. Navigation suites: no evidence they reduce wrong-level surgery and no verified capital cost (confirmed; level-counting-visual-abstract and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Record the search behind the absence claims (databases, terms, date) before the textbook carries them; otherwise carry them as 'not found by the authors'.
283. Regulatory status of phantom and cadaver work (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Cite the Body Bequest Program's research policy (or the WSU IRB's guidance on decedent research) and get the program's current fee before the textbook carries either.
284. Research that may support an FDA submission is ineligible for exemption (confirmed; retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Cite the WSU IRB policy or the 21 CFR 56 provision behind the FDA-ineligibility rule, and the eProtocol help text for the limited-review categories.
285. Background paragraph on wrong-level surgery (registry, surveys, litigation, NPDB, Joint Commission) (confirmed; aidas-seed-grant-proposal and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Keep one master copy (the protocol's carries fuller citations). Correct the registry description and the 81.3% reading in both places at once.
286. Method paragraph: the only learned component is the detector (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). Carry the method description once, with the grant's note that the 0.6-body window is untested against manual step error.
287. Counting results table (834/834, 1,205/1,209, 6,234/6,255, 7,168/7,228, 99.31% of 5,385) (confirmed; counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy and aidas-seed-grant-proposal). Carry the grant's version, which has intervals and the sixth confirmation arm, cited to the manuscript in preparation.

Carry the figures once with the abstract's citations, and use the grant's wording 'intraoperative disorientation' for the 81% (see candidate 8).
290. Comparison to navigation: no verified capital cost, no outcome study, surgeon workload (confirmed; level-counting-visual-abstract and retrospective-fluoroscopy-protocol). 291. Older eProtocol draft of 15 September 2026 (confirmed; protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p and protocol-1-eprotocol-draft-mkmh7p). Carry the Retrospective Fluoroscopy Protocol's fields. ### Numbers

447. number of C-ArmLSpine cases scored in the five-fold lateral whole-frame evaluation (98.2%): or-readiness-ledger gives 148 cases, counting-from-the-sacrum gives 141 cases, 617 vertebrae (confirmed-contradiction). Both texts describe the same evaluation: lateral whole-frame C-ArmLSpine, five-fold CV by case, 98.2%/98.22% exact level, lower CI bound 95.7/95.71. The ledger says the CV ran over 148 cases, which matches its own split table (105/15/28 cases = 148); the sacrum page labels the same row 141 cases, 617 vertebrae. Both pages are dated 22 September 2026 and neither mentions the other's count or says it revises it. 141 could be a subset of 148 (cases with at least one scored vertebra after truth filtering), but neither text says so, so the two stand as different denominators for one result. The sacrum page's separate 'C-ArmLSpine 120' is distinct CT subjects in the census, a different count again.
448. exact-level naming on a whole lateral C-ArmLSpine frame: or-readiness-ledger gives 98.2%, research-directions-from-the-data-holding-kcyckb gives 97.4% (confirmed-contradiction). Settle from paper 1's results table which figure is whole-frame naming. Carry 97.4% as the later whole-frame figure, and 98.2% as the coupled count only if that table confirms it.
449. exact-level naming with one vertebra in the lateral C-arm field: counting-from-the-sacrum gives 72.85%, handoff-schwingnet-ctspinopelvic1k-and-the-imaging-programme-rbcdmf gives 74.8% (confirmed-contradiction). Carry the figure from paper 1's final results table with its run named. If 74.8% is from the later model, say that the September pages carried 72.85%.
450. sacrum recall on real DeepFluoro frames, detector trained on renders of DeepFluoro's own CTs: aidas-seed-grant-proposal gives 96-98%, counting-levels-under-fluoroscopy gives 90-97% (confirmed-contradiction). Carry 90-97% (six specimens, three seeds each) from Counting Levels Under Fluoroscopy, which states its design and reports the later refiner results. Either correct the proposal and the retrospective protocol to it, or name the run that gave 96-98%.

## Cluster by cluster

Each swept cluster has a page of its own under contradictions/, linked from the table above: every candidate with its kind, both artifacts, whether a script found the quotes, the verdict and the skeptic's explanation, then what each artifact says, the clustering's superseded relations as the reader checked them, and what the cluster asserts without a source or holds as dated.
